For a thousand years, mariners confidently guided ships across oceans, and astronomers predicted lunar eclipses and planetary motion with jewelry-like precision. Throughout this, theoretical science ironcladly asserted: the Earth is the motionless center of the Universe, while the Sun and the stars revolve around it upon crystalline spheres. Ptolemy’s formulas, with their insane mathematical crutches ("epicycles"), functioned perfectly, delivering correct practical results based upon a root false theory.

**•	Example 2. Firearms and Ballistics:**  
In the Middle Ages, artillerymen constructed powerful cannons and calculated the trajectories of cannonballs based upon the theory of Aristotle. The physics of that era asserted that a cannonball flies forward because it is "pushed by the very air that it has itself pushed aside," and at the end of its trajectory, it falls strictly vertically downward. The cannons fired, cities collapsed, and applied engineering was successful, yet the theoretical explanation of the process was pure unadulterated nonsense.

**•	Example 3. Steam Engines and the Caloric Theory:**  
The Industrial Revolution, which overturned the world in the 18th and 19th centuries, was built upon the steam engines of James Watt. Trains rolled, and factories began to operate. And what did theoretical physics declare? It asserted that heat was a specific, massless fluid ("caloric") that flows from one body to another. The design of engines leaned upon the mathematical formulas of a non-existent fluid, which did not hinder engineers from constructing functioning locomotives.

The present day differs in no way from the past. When you are told that the GPS system functions exclusively due to "relativistic time dilation" and the curvature of spacetime, you are being fed yet another portion of mathematical abstractions. The engineering reality is such that the atomic clocks on navigation satellites indeed alter their rate.  
However, they alter it not because "time slows down," but because the satellite hurtles through a material cosmic medium with different parameters of density and pressure, which purely mechanically influences the oscillations of the elements inside the clocks.

Engineering invariably deals with the tangible physical properties of a medium, bodies, and waves. Yet theoretical physics, parasitizing upon the successes of engineers, has managed to construct an insane world out of formulas and mathematics around these practical data. We have been forced to believe that numbers on paper are capable of birthing matter, curving a void, and inflating space.

In this chapter, we shall strip modern cosmology of its aura of untouchable sanctity. We shall not distort the views of official science — we shall quote them in their pristine form. However, we shall pass these dogmas through the sieve of harsh geometric logic and simple, terrestrial common sense. And we shall commence from the very beginning — with modern physics’ attempt to compress infinity into an infinitesimal geometric zero.

**Part 1. The Mathematical Dead End at t = 0 (The Singularity)**

The official cosmological chronology commences with a mesmerizing and chilling word — "singularity". If you open any modern textbook, popular science article, or pick up a documentary film, you will be authoritatively told: "13.8 billion years ago, the Universe was in a state of singularity. All matter, all energy, and space itself were compressed into a single point with zero volume and infinite density. And then, the Big Bang occurred." 

They tell you this with such unwavering confidence, as if physicists had personally measured this point with a ruler. But let us switch on harsh geometric logic and see what actually hides behind this beautiful term.

What is a point in geometry? It is an abstract object that possesses no dimensions: neither length, nor width, nor height. A point has no volume. Its diameter is equal to a strict geometric zero.  
Now, attempt to translate this into the language of physical reality. Science invites us to believe that quadrillions of tons of tangible matter — all future stars, planets, galaxies, and you and I — were physically located inside an object that has no volume.

If we attempt to calculate the density of such an object using the simplest formula (mass divided by volume), then upon dividing by zero, we obtain infinity. Infinite density in a zero volume — this is precisely what a "singularity" represents.

But wait, in the real physical world, there is no such thing as "infinite density," just as there is no "infinite temperature" or "infinite mass". Any infinity in a physical result is not a description of a real object; it is direct evidence that the mathematical formulas have broken down.

When Einstein’s General Relativity reaches the moment t = 0, its equations begin to divide by zero and yield a mathematical absurdity. Instead of honestly admitting, "Our formulas are imperfect, they have hit a dead end and cannot describe this stage of reality," theoretical physics committed a fraud. It declared this bug in the equations to be an actual physical state of the Universe. The word "singularity" was simply used to cover up the intellectual impotence of mathematical models.

Realizing that a "zero volume" too severely insults the ears of any person possessing common sense, science immediately utilizes a "crutch" from quantum mechanics — the so-called Planck scale.   
We are told: "Fine, let us assume the volume was not entirely zero. The Universe was compressed to the Planck length — approximately 10<sup>-35</sup> meters. This is infinitesimally small, quadrillions of times smaller than a protn, yet it is still not a zero."

However, this crutch does not salvage the geometry; it merely compounds the absurdity. The density of the Universe at this stage still turns out to be monstrous — 10<sup>93</sup> grams per cubic centimeter. To help you comprehend the scale of this fantasy: into a single cubic centimeter (the size of a sugar cube), theoretical physics manages to "pack" the mass of our entire immense Observable Universe with its trillions of galaxies.

Any person who has retained the capability to think understands: an object with an infinitesimally small (or zero) diameter is fundamentally incapable of containing an infinite volume of real substance. Either substance is material and occupies a location in space, or you are engaged in pure mathematical mysticism.

Yet the most astonishing part commences further on. According to the assurances of cosmologists, inside this "pea" of negligible diameter resided not only future atoms. Within it, at that very same moment, 26 independent quantum fields concurrently managed to conflict-lessly coexist, waiting for their hour to construct our world.

**Part 2. The Fields That Were "Sleeping" Within the Void**

While our microscopic Universe of Planckian diameter was preparing for its blast, a quiet quantum drama was unfolding inside it, if official science is to be believed. Theoretical physics asserts that at this ultra-early instant, not a single real particle yet existed in space — no quarks, no electrons, and no photons of light. Yet despite this, all 26 fundamental quantum fields predicted by the Standard Model were already present within it in full complement.

How is this to be understood? In quantum field theory, we are taught that a field is an invisible ocean permeating the cosmos, while a particle is merely a wave or a splash upon this ocean. The electron field births electrons, the quark field births quarks, and the electromagnetic field births light (photons).

And so, science offers us the following picture: matter does not yet exist, but 26 invisible oceans are already ironcladly woven into reality. Moreover, all of them are packed inside that very same negligible point of Planckian dimensions. To salvage logic, physicists invented another term — the "ground or vacuum state of fields." 

This implies that the average energy of all these 25 fields (except the inflaton field) was strictly equal to zero. The fields were absolutely tranquil; there was not a single wave nor a single quantum knot upon them. Like obedient empty glasses, they were arranged upon the infinitesimally small table of the singularity and meekly awaited the command to "turn on."

Yet let us switch on terrestrial geometry once more and ask this theory point-blank: if these fields are the fundamental fabric of reality, of what then did the borders of the point itself consist?

If 26 fields occupied the exact same negligible volume of 10<sup>-35</sup> meters, how then did they physically separate from one another? At the Planck scale, wavelength runs into an absolute physical dead end — everything there possesses one and the same minimal dimension. 26 independent layers of reality, compressed into a single point, were mathematically obligated to merge into an indivisible chaos.

However, the most paradoxical aspect is the relationship of these fields to the void. Science asserts that beyond the boundaries of our Planckian point, absolutely nothing existed — neither space nor time. But if space around the point did not exist, where then did these 26 fields terminate? Does it turn out that the electron or magnetic fields possessed a clear, rigid boundary with a diameter a fraction of a proton's size, beyond which physical reality simply broke off into an absolute nothingness?

Here, theoretical physics commits a classic crime against logic: it endows its mathematical abstractions (fields) with the properties of rigid physical bodies when it needs to compress them into a point, yet instantly declares them "immaterial spirits" the moment the reader begins to pose inconvenient geometric questions regarding boundaries and dimensions.

Within this empty quantum tightness, only a single solitary field ruled indivisibly — the inflaton. It was precisely this field that accumulated within itself the entire colossal energy of the vacuum and acted as the chief detonator of the cosmos, triggering the process that science terms Cosmic Inflation.

**Part 3. The Inflaton — A Detonator Made of Numbers**

Before we dissect how the microscopic "pea" of the Universe inflated to the dimensions of a ten-meter house in an infinitesimal fraction of a second, we must expose the chief masking word of modern physics. This word is "energy."

In popular science films, energy is depicted as a certain glowing fluid, a cosmic plasma, or a bubbling force-liquid capable of pushing and exploding things. Yet in the real physical world, no material substance named "energy" exists. One cannot pour it into a glass or touch it. The great physicist Richard Feynman honestly admitted: "It is important to realize that in physics today, we have no knowledge of what energy is." 

**Energy is a purely mathematical counter, an abstract number** that scientists record in their equations for the convenience of calculations. It is a formula on paper. In and of itself, a formula is fundamentally incapable of physically pushing, compressing, or exploding anyone. **Only real material bodies can execute motion and exert pressure through contact interaction.**

Yet official cosmology completely ignores this fact. According to its version, when the Universe possessed a negligible Planckian diameter, there was no matter within it, yet it was ruled by the "colossal energy" of a hypothetical field — the inflaton.

Keeping in mind that no real energy can exist within a void, let us examine what mathematical miracle forced this inflaton to "explode" the Universe.  
Physicists endowed the inflaton field with a unique mathematical property — negative pressure. According to Einstein’s equations, if one substitutes a number with a minus sign into the formula for gravitation, the force of attraction miraculously transforms into its opposite — into a force of cosmic repulsion.

And so, obeying a formula with a negative sign, the inflaton triggers cosmic inflation. In an unfathomable 10<sup>-32</sup> seconds, the empty space of the Universe itself inflates by 10<sup>26</sup> times (and according to some models — by 10<sup>50</sup> or 10<sup>100</sup> times). Our future Observable Universe transforms from a sub-quantum point into a sphere the size of a three-story house (about 10 meters).

Notice: while this ten-meter sphere was inflating, it remained, according to the assurances of science, absolutely empty. There was not a single atom, not a single quark within it. Inside the house resided only a void obeying a mathematical acceleration function.

However, the most overt shamanism transpires at the finale of inflation, at the stage that cosmologists term "reheating." The inflaton field abruptly "turns off," its mathematical potential drops, and physics declares: "The energy of the inflaton collapsed in an avalanche-like manner and transformed into a bubbling soup of real particles—quarks, gluons, and electrons."

That is to say, dry numbers in equations describing an empty field materialized, with a slight sleight of hand by a theoretical physicist, into billions of billions of tons of physical substance.

From whence came this tangible mass? From what source? Science responds: from the "negative credit" of the gravitational field. We are literally told that the total energy of the Universe is invariably equal to zero: the positive mass of substance is precisely counterbalanced by the negative energy of gravitation.

Confronting us is a sleight of hand of the purest water: formulas on paper went into a mathematical "minus," and by virtue of this virtual debt, a real, ultra-hot quark-gluon plasma instantaneously boiled within the empty ten-meter sphere. Physical processes were substituted by the movement of numbers, which, without any mechanical contact whatsoever, created the material world out of nothing.

**The Mechanics of "Exciting" Empty Glasses**

To definitively comprehend the scale of this mathematical shamanism, one must delve into exactly how, in the opinion of official science, the inflaton filled the Universe with substance. It did not generate quarks, electrons, and photons directly from its own "body." The theory asserts that the oscillating inflaton field functioned as a certain cosmic tuning fork, which, through the mechanism of quantum resonance, transmitted its "energy" (that is, mathematical numbers) to the remaining twenty-five "sleeping" fields.

Recall the picture from Part Two: inside our empty ten-meter sphere, as if arranged upon a table, stand 25 absolutely empty "glasses" — the electron field, the quark fields, the photon field. Within them, there is not a single wave nor a single particle; their average value is equal to a strict zero. They are materially vacant.

And so, the inflaton begins to decay and frantically oscillate. By virtue of purely mathematical coupling formulas (the so-called interaction Lagrangians), these oscillations flow from one equation into others. And — lo and behold! — the empty, lifeless fields, which contained no matter whatsoever, suddenly begin to "excite" and vibrate.

•	Waves surge upon the electron field — electrons are born.    
•	Waves surge upon the quark fields — quarks are born.    
•	Ripples rise upon the photon field — light (photons) is born.

Yet let us switch on sane mechanics and ask: how can one excite that which does not exist in a physical sense? How can one transmit motion to a field that contains no material particles capable of receiving this motion?

In the real world, you can excite water in a glass only if you strike the glass itself with another material body — a contact transfer of motion will transpire. However, in the mad world of quantum cosmology, the immaterial mathematical formula of the inflaton, without having any physical contact whatsoever, excited other immaterial formulas, and out of this digital resonance of the void, a tangible, bubbling soup of matter suddenly wove itself.

We are forced to believe that empty quantum fields are a species of invisible strings that can resonate with real particles if they are properly "plucked" by the threads of equations. And this soup of massless particles, brought to a boil by mathematical excitation and born within a ten-meter house, continued its expansion further — toward the dimensions of the Solar System, where the next logical dead end already awaited it: the activation of the Higgs field.

**Part 4. The Higgs Phase Transition: A Solar System Without Mass**

Thus, our expanding blueberry pie of the Universe passed the mark of one trillionth of a second after the start. During this negligible instant, the soup of particles that had come to a boil within the ten-meter "house" inflated, by virtue of ordinary expansion, to a colossal volume — to the dimensions of the Solar System (billions of kilometers in diameter).

Yet this young cosmos differed drastically from our own. Inside it, there was not a single solid object, not a single atomic nucleus, and not one solitary planet. The reason is simple: inside this vast Universe the size of the Solar System, matter possessed no mass.

All the quarks and electrons born during the preceding epoch were absolutely massless. And since they possessed no mass, they were obligated, according to the laws of relativistic mathematics, to chaotically dash through space strictly at the speed of light.

Within this frantic luminous chaos, yet another astonishing theoretical miracle was transpiring: quarks failed to disturb the adjacent layer altogether — the gluon field responsible for the strong nuclear interaction. Why did the gluons not interfere in the life of the quarks and make no attempt to glue them together? Official science responds to this with the term "asymptotic freedom". 

Physicists wrote a formula according to which, at ultra-close distances and at luminous velocities, the force of nuclear adhesion drops to zero. The quarks dash in close proximity to one another, yet the gluon field remains indifferent because mathematical crutches have temporarily blocked its operation, preventing the Universe from transforming into a single continuous gravitational thrombus ahead of time.  
However, the mark of 10<sup>-12</sup> seconds arrives, and the chief mystical character of the Standard Model steps onto the stage — the Higgs field.

Prior to this moment, the Higgs field was sleeping meekly. Why did it wait and fail to activate earlier? Science explains this via "high temperature." We are told that within the ultra-hot plasma, the thermal energy of the vacuum was so vast that it sustained the potential of the Higgs field in an ideal symmetry equal to a strict zero. Yet as soon as the volume expanded to the scale of the Solar System, the plasma cooled to the critical mark of 100 GeV (10<sup>15</sup> Kelvin), and the Higgs field instantaneously executed the so-called "electroweak phase transition" — it froze and "turned on".

Let us switch on logic: what does it signify that "the field reacted to cooling and froze"? Out of what did this "syrup" materialize in the first place? Physical cooling and a phase transition (for instance, the transformation of water into ice) are possible exclusively within material media consisting of particles. For a medium to cool down, its constituent elements must decrease the velocity of their thermal vibrations.

Yet of what does the Higgs field itself consist? Science asserts that it is fundamental, continuous, and consists of no particles whatsoever. If a field possesses no internal structure, what exactly inside it cooled and froze? An immaterial mathematical canvas cannot possess its own temperature. We are invited to believe in a miracle: an empty property of space "felt the cold" entirely on its own and altered its mathematical parameters without any physical mechanism whatsoever.

Upon activating, this field instantaneously transformed into an invisible "viscous syrup" filling the entire gigantic volume of the Solar System. The massless quarks and electrons flying at the speed of light began to "rub" against this newly manifested mathematical syrup, their velocity dropped, and formulas endowed them with the property we term mass. Only after this were the decelerated electrons capable of hooking onto the quarks to construct the first atoms in the future.

And here we stumble upon the next, utterly fantastical crutch. Since the moment the Higgs field activated, the Universe has expanded by billions of billions of times — from the dimensions of the Solar System to an un-envisageable infinity. Any real material medium (a gas, a liquid, a foam), during such an expansion, is obligated to thin out, losing its density and concentration. If you stretch a syrup across an immense room, it will transform into an invisible vapor.

Yet with the Higgs field, this failed to transpire! Space inflated colossally, but the density of the Higgs "syrup" inside every single cubic centimeter of the Universe remained strictly immutable. The field miraculously preserved its properties at 100%. The mass of an electron on Earth today is precisely the same as it was billions of years ago inside that tiny Solar System. 

Science assures us that the fabric of the Higgs field is capable of infinitely stretching without losing concentration and without requiring new substance to fill it. This is not physics — it is pure mathematical idealism, where numbers on paper possess the property of eternal immutability, transmitting this immutability to the structure of the field.  
And this immutable "syrup," frozen within the void, guided the expanding cosmos further — toward the geometric dead end of official cosmology, toward the paradox of an infinite garland of "beads."

**Part 5. The Great Paradox of "Beads" (A Flat, Infinite Universe)**

A Crucial Digression: What does "flat" signify if stars are visible from all directions?
Before advancing further, let us once and for all annihilate the chief terminological misunderstanding within cosmology. When official science declares that our Universe is "flat," it does not mean that the cosmos resembles a pancake, a two-dimensional sheet of paper, or a pizza. Stars and galaxies surround us absolutely from all sides, receding into the depth of a three-dimensional space. No one disputes this.

The word "flat" in physics describes not the shape of the Universe, but its internal geometry. It means that within cosmic space, the ordinary, straight laws of school Euclidean geometry operate perfectly.

To comprehend the difference between a flat and a curved three-dimensional space, visualize three simple tests:

**1.	The Laser Test:**    
If you activate two powerful lasers in deep space strictly parallel to one another, within our flat Universe their beams will travel for billions of years and invariably remain parallel. Conversely, were space curved (spherical), these beams would eventually, entirely on their own, begin to draw closer and intersect, even though no one had deflected them.

**2.	The Triangle Test:**    
If you mentally draw a gigantic triangle between three distant galaxies, then within our flat cosmos, the sum of its angles will be strictly equal to 180°. Within a curved cosmos, it would be either greater or less than 180°.

**3.	An Everyday Example:**    
Visualize an ordinary room inside your apartment. The walls, the floor, and the ceiling all around constitute a 3D volume; you can look in absolutely any direction. Yet, the corners in the room are right angles, the floor is level, and the ceiling is flat. Light within it travels along straight lines.

Our Universe is exactly such a room, only infinite and devoid of walls. Substance fills it across all three dimensions, which is why telescopes observe stars in any direction. However, the space itself inside this three-dimensional "room" is ideally level and uncurbed by global gravitation.

Now, having liberated the word "flat" from any associations with a kitchen pancake and having understood that we are dealing with an ideally level three-dimensional geometric grid, let us return to our primary question: how did this level, infinite grid manage to be born out of a negligible point?

To describe the scale of today's Universe, official science is forced to tear itself apart between two fundamentally different geometric models, in both of which a negligibly small Planckian diameter invariably figures at the starting line.

**Version 1. The Simplified "Solitary Bead" (For the Mass Consumer)**

This is the variant you constantly witness on television screens, in popular science books, and on YouTube. A beautiful and simple picture is drawn: there existed a single, solitary, isolated microscopic "pea" (a bead). Around it, there was absolutely nothing—neither space nor time. Then, the inflaton inflated this specific bead to the dimensions of a "house," after which it grew to the size of the "Solar System," and today it has transformed into an immense yet finite three-dimensional sphere that possesses a clear geometric volume and somewhere deep in the past had a single center of the blast.

This model is understandable to the human mind, yet it possesses one massive problem: official academic science rejects it. The solitary expanding sphere was left in textbooks exclusively for the sake of simplicity of visualization, because graphically rendering what actually transpired on a television screen is fundamentally impossible.

**Version 2. The Infinite Garland (The Dominant View of Science)**

What, then, does the official mainstream of cosmology declare based upon the data from the WMAP and Planck space telescopes? Scientists measured the curvature of our space with jewelry-like precision and delivered a verdict: the Universe is ideally flat. And within Einsteinian geometry, a strictly flat cosmos without boundaries can mathematically be only infinite.

Yet if the Universe is infinite today, then by the laws of geometry it was obligated to be infinite always — both a second ago, and at the moment of the Higgs field activation, and at the very first instant of the Big Bang.  
And it is precisely here that the theory dominant in science (the Chaotic Inflation model) rolls out its true geometric structure to us, making one's hair stand on end.

At the moment of the Big Bang, the Universe was not a single, solitary point. It consisted of a countless multitude of infinitesimally small points — "beads" — that were pressed flush against one another, without gaps or voids, into an infinite three-dimensional network receding to the right, to the left, upward, and downward without end or boundary. 

One of these negligible beads is our future habitable world (everything we observe in telescopes today). Yet to the left and to the right of it sat trillions of exactly the same independent neighbor-beads. And they extended not merely far away, but infinitely. A countless multitude of "points" in an infinity of distance.

Now, watch the sleight of hand of the theoretical physicists. When the general inflaton field issues the command to "blast," this process is triggered simultaneously throughout the entire infinite network.

•	Our negligible bead, in a fraction of a second, inflates into a 10-meter house.    
•	The neighboring bead on the right, at that very same instant, inflates into an identical 10-meter house.    
•	The bead behind it does the same.

This entire infinite garland of "houses" begins to concurrently and violently swell at every point of infinite space.  
And it is precisely here that real geometry ironcladly jams the scientific formulas. Let us switch on our spatial imagination: if you possess an infinite plane tightly packed with touching microscopic cells, and every single cell begins to expand from its internal center to the dimensions of a house, and subsequently to the dimensions of the Solar System — what must transpire with their boundaries?

In the real geometric world, these neighboring, expanding "houses" were obligated to instantaneously crash into one another, crumple their boundaries, squeeze their neighbors, and transform the entire infinite Universe into a monstrous traffic jam of overlapping matter and fields.

To salvage the formulas from this obvious collapse, cosmologists invented yet another terminological incantation. We are told: "No, they did not crash, because they did not execute motion toward one another through space. Their space is shared, the canvas is one, but the very ground beneath them was expanding. The very dough of the pie was being birthed between them, which is why the boundaries simply receded smoothly." 

Confronting us once again is a pure game of words. If the canvas of space is shared, the fields are shared, and the nodes of this canvas violently expand, increasing their internal diameters, then the geometry of spatial tightness is relentless: objects are obligated to interact via their boundaries. 

Yet mathematical cosmology simply closes its eyes to this dead end. It forces us to believe in an infinite network of "beads" that manage to inflate from points to the scales of Solar Systems flush against one another, without generating any traffic jams or mechanical collisions, simply because the scale factor in the Friedmann equations alters with a slight stroke of a pen.

**The Trap of Random Fluctuations**

However, let us trap official science with its own chief word — with the concept of a "quantum fluctuation." Physicists assert that the inflaton field did not merely explode on its own; its decay was triggered by a random quantum fluctuation — a spontaneous microscopic surge within the vacuum.

And it is precisely here that the trap of logic snaps shut definitively. A quantum fluctuation, by its very nature, is a strictly local, chaotic event. It transpires randomly, at a specific microscopic location, rather than by order of a generalissimo throughout the entire infinity simultaneously.

Theoretical physics is confronted by a harsh fork of two scenarios, and both of them completely annihilate common sense:

**Scenario A. Mystical Simultaneity**

If science insists that the Big Bang occurred inside all the countless beads of the infinite Universe at one and the same instant, it implies that the fluctuation miraculously transpired everywhere second-for-second across trillions of light-years around. Yet this is statistically impossible! Inside an infinite, raging ocean, random surges cannot occur synchronously. To assert that the infinite inflaton field decayed everywhere simultaneously is not physics — it is pure mysticism, devoid of any signal transmission mechanism.

**Scenario B. Chaotic Blasts (Linde’s Official Mainstream)**

Realizing this dead end, the currently dominant theory of Eternal Inflation declares: "Yes, the blasts transpire at different times! The inflaton boils eternally; inside our bead, the fluctuation went off 13.8 billion years ago, whereas inside our neighbors' beads, it will occur in trillions of years, or has not occurred at all yet. Surrounding us is a multitude of unexploded, sleeping Planckian points." 

Yet now, let us switch on real geometry and examine what this official mainstream executes to our ideally flat Universe.  
Visualize this: our "bead" exploded and, in an infinitesimal fraction of a second, inflated from a Planckian point first into a 10-meter house, and subsequently into the scale of the Solar System. Meanwhile, our neighbor on the right failed to explode. It remained sleeping in the state of a negligible Planckian pixel with a dimension of 10<sup>-35</sup> meters, awaiting its own fluctuation.

How can our expanding, gigantic volume, filled with bubbling plasma, border a neighbor that remained a microscopic point? In the real geometric world, our expanding bubble was obligated to literally crush, erase, or absorb this unborn neighbor.

Furthermore, by the laws of General Relativity, space remains flat exclusively when density within it is distributed uniformly. If inside our bead the energy density is colossal, while inside the neighbor's it equals zero, such a monstrous differential will arise at their boundary that the fabric of spacetime in that location was obligated to curve to infinity, twist into a dead loop, and tear to shreds. The infinite Universe would have transformed into a tattered lace of spatial anomalies and black holes.

Yet the Planck and WMAP space telescopes stubbornly demonstrate to us an ideally level, smooth, and flat cosmos completely devoid of any seams, tears, or geometric defects.

Official science has driven itself into a dead end: to salvage the randomness of fluctuations, it is forced to disperse the blasts of the beads over time. However, by doing so, it completely annihilates the flat geometry of the Universe, which it itself proved via observations. Formulas on paper triumphed over reality once more: cosmologists simply prohibited expanding bubbles from colliding with sleeping neighbors, concealing this geometric collapse behind the beautiful term "Multiverse."

**The Optical Prison of Our "Bead"**

To definitively comprehend the entire paradox of the official infinite garland of beads model, pose to scientists one simple question: "If at the moment of the Big Bang, trillions of other identical neighbor-points sat flush around our Planckian point, and all of them exploded simultaneously, why then do we not witness them in the night sky? Where is the light from their stars and galaxies?"

And it is precisely here that official science rolls out its most astounding admission, which completely alters our understanding of the word "Universe." Cosmologists state: "Absolutely everything you observe in telescopes is strictly the boundaries of our own bead alone."

Our entire so-called Observable Universe—this colossal habitable bubble which, over 13.8 billion years of expansion, has grown to a diameter of 93 billion light-years — is not the entire cosmos. It is merely the inflated innards of our single, solitary former Planckian point.

•	All the trillions of galaxies captured by the Hubble and James Webb space telescopes.    
•	All the substance from which the Sun, the Earth, and you and I are constructed.    
•	This entire ocean of stars in every single direction.

All of this represents the thinnest "splinters and shards" that once came to a boil inside our local ten-meter inflationary "house" and grew along with it.

But where are the neighbors?  
The neighboring bead-points that, at the instant of 10<sup>-36</sup> seconds, were in contact with ours, have today inflated in exactly the same manner into their own, independent 93-billion-light-year bubbles. Yet witnessing them from Earth is physically impossible. Due to the fact that the shared space itself (the "dough of the pie") between the centers of our points has, from the very first instant of inflation, expanded at superluminal velocity, our former neighbors have hurtled away from us beyond the boundary of any conceivable physical reach. Between our "bubbles," space has grown and continues to grow.

Visualize an alien who ignited a colossal cosmic searchlight inside a neighboring bead adjacent to us. This light honestly travels in our direction through the shared space at the maximum possible physical velocity — 300,000 kilometers per second. However, the distance between our bubbles increases faster, due to the birth of new "dough," than this light travels. The photons of the alien universe stubbornly travel forward, yet space itself carries them backward, distancing them from our visible Universe, from the Earth.

This alien light is locked behind an absolutely impenetrable, invisible wall that physicists term the cosmic particle horizon. Space and fields out there, beyond this wall, continue infinitely, yet for us, they are dead. Not a single photon, not a single signal, and not one solitary physical object from neighboring points will ever be capable of breaching this superluminal expansion blockade.

Look at the fantastical optical prison into which official theory drives us. We are painted a picture of a countless multitude of concurrently exploded worlds, united by a shared space and shared 26 quantum fields. Yet it immediately transpires that this entire infinite garland exists exclusively on paper in the form of mathematical formulas.

Experimentally verifying the existence of even a single neighboring bead is fundamentally impossible. We are eternally locked inside our own inflated "pea," surrounded on all sides by a wall of absolute nothingness, and are forced to take the Friedmann equations at their word, accepting the mathematical apparatus of theorists as the actual design of the cosmos.

**Part 6. Inflation — The Story of One Saving Crutch**

To comprehend why modern physics found it necessary to assert that an empty Universe once inflated at a velocity multiplying the speed of light, one must peer behind the curtains of scientific history. Physicists devised cosmic inflation in the early 1980s (Alan Guth and Andrei Linde distinguished themselves here) not because they discovered some new phenomenon of nature. They were forced into this by a severe crisis in the classical Big Bang theory. The old model stumbled over three fundamental paradoxes that were logically impossible to explain.

**Paradox 1. The Horizon Problem (Why is everything around us identical?)**

No matter where we look today through billions of light-years — to the "left" or to the "right" — the Universe looks absolutely identical everywhere. The cosmic microwave background (the ancient thermal background) possesses an identical temperature at all points of the sky with a precision down to thousandths of a percent.

**•	Where the absurdity lies:**  
By the laws of the classical Big Bang, these opposite edges of the cosmos were scattering apart so rapidly that light and heat physically would never have had enough time to travel from one edge to the other. They never interacted. How then did they manage to "agree" and cool down to an absolutely identical temperature? This is equivalent to pouring hot and cold water into a gigantic swimming pool from different sides, instantaneously expanding the banks by kilometers, and discovering that the water everywhere became ideally warm entirely on its own, without any mixing.

**Paradox 2. The Flatness Problem**

We already know that our Universe is ideally level and Euclidean. Yet, for it to remain flat over the course of 13.8 billion years, at the very first instant its density had to be equal to the critical density with a fantastical, non-human precision — down to the sixtieth decimal place. An error equivalent to the weight of a single grain of sand in one direction or the other would have caused the cosmos either to instantaneously collapse back into a black hole or to scatter apart so rapidly that atoms would not have had time to form. Science could not explain who had so finely "tuned" this balance.

**Paradox 3. The Magnetic Monopole Problem**

All quantum equations predicted that the hot Big Bang should have birthed a countless multitude of super-heavy particles — magnetic monopoles (magnets with a single pole). There should have been so many of them that they would dominate the cosmos. Yet, astronomers have failed to find a single one.

**How Inflation Resolved Everything "in One Fell Swoop" (On Paper)**

To patch these three puncture wounds, physicists devised inflation — a stage of ultra-fast, exponential inflation of empty space. 

**•	The Solution to the Horizon Problem:**    
Prior to inflation, our "bead" was so tiny (smaller than a proton) that all substance within it had enough time to perfectly mix and arrive at a single temperature. And then, inflation instantaneously tore this already uniform droplet apart, carrying its edges away at superluminal velocity.

**•	The Solution to the Flatness Problem:**    
Visualize a severely wrinkled, curved toy balloon. If it is instantaneously inflated to the dimensions of the Earth, its surface will appear ideally flat to any ant walking upon it. Inflation simply "smoothed out" the geometry of space, concealing any curvature beyond the horizon of visibility.

**•	The Solution to the Monopole Problem:**    
Inflation expanded the volume of space so monstrously that all the born monopoles were simply "diluted" within this infinity. For our entire immense Observable Universe, at most one monopole remained, which is why we fail to witness it.

Look at this tactic: instead of searching for real mechanical causes for the uniformity and flatness of the material world, theoretical physics simply invented a hypothetical field (the inflaton), endowed it with the magical property of "negative pressure," and forced the void itself to expand faster than light, in order to plaster over all the logical holes of its theory under the microscope of formulas. Inflation is not a triumph of discovery; it is a grandiose mathematical band-aid applied to a broken cosmology of the Big Bang.

To ensure that you definitively fix this fantastical process in your mind and do not become entangled in its scales, let us once more clearly log its dry spacetime coordinates:

**•	The Start Time of Inflation:**    
Exactly 10<sup>-36</sup> seconds after the mythical instant of singularity. At this moment, our future Observable Universe (our "bead") was a sub-quantum point smaller than the Planck length — smaller than 10<sup>-35</sup> meters. Adjacent to it, trillions of identical neighbor-"beads" were pressed flush, and all of this occupied an infinite space. Infinity was filled with this "minutia."

**•	The End Time of Inflation:**    
Between 10<sup>-33</sup> and 10<sup>-32</sup> seconds from the beginning of time. The entire process occupied an infinitesimal, unfathomable fraction of a second — one trillionth of a trillionth of a second.

**•	To What Dimension the Volume of the Beads Grew:**    
During this instant, every empty "bead" (including our own) increased its scale by at least 10<sup>26</sup> times and inflated to the dimensions of a large apple or a three-story house (about 10 meters in diameter), depending on the radicalism of the mathematical model. The infinite network of these "houses" grew simultaneously in all directions.

**•	At What Velocity the Expansion Proceeded:**    
To pack such a colossal growth into such a minuscule fraction of a second, the very fabric of the Universe's space was obligated to expand at a velocity that multiplied — by trillions of times — the speed of light.

Official science declares without a shred of embarrassment that the empty Universe breached the light barrier in a fraction of a second, because Einstein’s laws prohibit traveling faster than light exclusively for matter moving through space, whereas for the expansion of space itself, no speed limits exist.

And so, when inflation ground to a halt at the mark of 10<sup>-32</sup> seconds, and the empty ten-meter "house" of our bead instantaneously boiled with quark-gluon plasma due to the decay of the inflaton, the Universe transitioned to the stage of ordinary, inertial expansion.  
During the subsequent interval of time — from 10<sup>-32</sup> to 10<sup>-12</sup> seconds — this ten-meter "house" smoothly expanded to the dimensions of the Solar System (billions of kilometers in diameter) where, as we already know, the cooled massless soup of particles was decelerated by the activated Higgs field.

Now, keeping in mind this rigid scale — from the Planckian point (10<sup>-35</sup> meters) through the ten-meter house (10<sup>-32</sup> seconds) to the scale of the Solar System (10<sup>-12</sup> seconds) and, finally, to today’s billions of light-years — we are ready to transition to the finale of our chronology, where the expanding pie of the Universe presents us with its chief velocity paradox.

**Part 7. The Velocity Illusion of the "Blueberry Pie"**

Having traversed the path from the Planckian point, through the ten-meter house of inflation and the billions of kilometers of the massless Solar System (10<sup>-12</sup> seconds), we finally arrive at our contemporary era. Following 13.8 billion years of expansion, our local habitable sector — our former "bead" — has expanded into a colossal spherical bubble with a diameter of 93 billion light-years. This is what science terms our Observable Universe.

And it is precisely here that official physics offers us a sophisticated velocity paradox, which definitively substitutes real physical motion with mathematical manipulations.  
If you look at distant galaxies located on the "periphery" of our 93-billion-light-year sphere, official astronomy — on the basis of Hubble's Law — will authoritatively declare to you: "These galaxies are flying away from Earth at superluminal velocity — faster than 300,000 kilometers per second." 

Keeping in mind that Einstein allegedly ironcladly prohibited any material body from exceeding the speed of light, we hear once again the already familiar cosmological incantation: "The law is not violated! Galaxies are not flying through space. They are motionlessly embedded within it, like blueberries in an expanding pie. The very dough of the pie is expanding — space itself is being born between you, which is why the cumulative velocity of their recession exceeds the speed of light."

Now let us set aside this verbal acrobatics and ask the theory point-blank: what is the true, real velocity of these galaxies' motion in the physical world?

In everyday logic, everything is simple: if an object is receding from us at superluminal velocity, it implies that its engines or some force accelerated it to this insane speed. Yet in quantum cosmology, reality is mirrored and flipped upside down.

Physicists have learned to measure the velocity of any object relative to the "dough of the pie" itself (relative to the fabric of spacetime). As an absolute cosmic reference point, they utilize the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) — that very same primordial thermal background that uniformly fills the entire cosmos. And if one measures the velocity of those identical "superluminal" galaxies relative to this background, we obtain a staggering result.

Their real velocity of motion through space is merely a few hundred kilometers per second. By cosmic standards, they move at a snail's pace — exactly like our Earth, which drifts through the CMB background at a velocity of approximately 370 kilometers per second. The alien astronomers over there sit in their observatories, their stars peacefully revolve around the center of their galaxy, and no light barriers inside their world are being breached.

Look at the logical dead end into which official science drives us due to its attachment to the relativity of coordinates. For us, this distant galaxy is hurtling at superluminal velocity. Yet for an observer inside that very galaxy, their world is almost motionless, whereas our Earth is rushing away from them at superluminal velocity.

Everything in the Universe is concurrently "standing" in place and rushing at a velocity exceeding the speed of light — depending entirely on which "bead" of the infinite garland you decide to look at the blueprint from. The physical motion of bodies, their momentum, and their contact interaction have completely dissolved into the geometry of the expanding "dough."

However, the most beautiful optical trick lies in the fact that we witness these superluminal galaxies right now in our telescopes. It would seem that if they are fleeing faster than light, the light emitted by them ought to be carried backward — like a bullet fired against the direction of a train’s motion — and never reach the Earth.

The official crutch explaining this miracle is structured as follows: the light we capture today (for instance, via the James Webb space telescope) was launched billions of years ago. In that ancient epoch, the Universe was smaller, and these galaxies were located much closer to us. The velocity of space expansion between us back then was still subluminal, and the photons managed to escape forward.

For billions of years, they traveled through the expanding canvas; they were carried backw ard, yet they stubbornly advanced forward, gradually transitioning to those sectors of the cosmic "dough" that are closer to the Earth. And the closer to the Earth, the slower the space itself expands. Ultimately, the light broke out of the "superluminal trap" and fell upon our sensors.

We are looking at the ancient ghost of a galaxy, broadcasting its subluminal past to us. Yet its present is already cut off from us forever: the light it is emitting right now, in 2026, due to the acceleration of the Universe by dark energy, will never be capable of overcoming the expansion of space and will fly off into the infinite darkness.

Official cosmology has constructed a world in which real physical objects — galaxies consisting of billions of material stars — have transformed into virtual ghosts whose velocity, dimension, and very existence depend exclusively on how mathematicians recalculate the scale factor in the Friedmann equations. Numbers on paper turned out to be more powerful than the real motion of matter.

**Part 8. The Clash of Ghosts: The Dark "Who-Knows-What" vs. Gravitation**

Thus, our Universe has survived to the mark of 13.8 billion years. From a negligible Planckian point, through the stage of a ten-meter inflationary "house," and the scale of the massless "Solar System," it has expanded into an un-envisageable ocean of galaxies.

And today, attempting to calculate what will become of this colossal pie next, official science clashes in a mortal combat two of the chief ghosts of its theory: the warping force of Gravitation and the enigmatic Dark Energy.

Let us closely scrutinize this "dark energy." What is it? Of what does it consist? What is its material composition? The response of official academic science sounds honest: we do not know. Physicists believe that dark energy constitutes approximately 68% of the entire contents of the Universe, yet humanity possesses not the slightest concept of its physical nature. It lacks particles, it cannot be captured in a laboratory, and it neither emits nor absorbs light.

And here, theoretical physics rolls out once more its favorite saving incantation, utilizing the word "energy." We have already established that energy is simply a mathematical formula, a number on paper. By naming an unknown cosmic phenomenon "energy," science merely masked the phrase: "We observe that space expands with acceleration, but we do not understand the mechanics of the process, so we shall substitute into the equations a constant number with the sign we require."

The strangest geometric property of this "who-knows-what" lies in its density. When ordinary matter (gas, stars) expands, its density drops. Yet the density of dark energy inside every single cubic centimeter of space remains strictly constant since the beginning of time. The Universe has grown by billions of times, but inside every single newly born kilometer of void, a strictly fixed portion of dark energy materializes instantaneously and out of nowhere. It does not thin out.

Depending on exactly how this mathematical "who-knows-what" behaves in the future during its clash with the force of gravitation, official science outlines three scenarios of the finale for all our countless neighbor-"beads" simultaneously:

**Scenario 1. The Big Freeze (Thermal Death and Total Isolation)**

This is the mainstream of modern cosmology. It will operate if the density of dark energy within space remains strictly immutable.

•	First, in 100 billion years, dark energy will drag the "neighboring houses"-universes so far apart that the velocity of their recession will definitively exceed the speed of light, and they will depart forever beyond the horizon of our visibility. Our habitable bubble will be left in absolute loneliness.

•	Yet the stretching will not grind to a halt there. By the laws of this theory, the cosmos will continue to inflate with acceleration, and new kilometers of void will begin to violently generate already inside our very own bubble. Space between clusters of galaxies will stretch across monstrous, un-envisageable distances. All the neighboring galaxies that we witness in the sky today will hurtle so far away that their light will no longer be capable of breaching through to us.

•	Ultimately, the expansion of space will overcome the gravitational bonds inside the galactic systems themselves. The fabric of spacetime will begin to push objects apart inside the Milky Way. Stars, planetary systems, and gas clouds will find themselves torn away from one another. The distances between them will become so gigantic that every cooling star, every planet, and every asteroid will transform into absolutely lonely, isolated islands, lost within an infinitely growing, icy void.

•	In 10<sup>100</sup> years, when the final red dwarfs burn out and black holes slowly evaporate via Hawking radiation, the Universe will transform into an infinite, uniform, dead ocean, where for trillions of kilometers around there will be nothing except rare photons and electrons executing motion in complete loneliness.


**Scenario 2. The Big Rip (The Phantom Catastrophe)**

The most violent geometric scenario. It is possible if dark energy turns out to be "phantom" — meaning its power begins to progressively grow over time.

•	In approximately 20 to 50 billion years, the force of this mathematical pushing apart will become so monstrous that it will overcome the gravitation of galaxies and tear the Milky Way to shreds.

•	Three months prior to the finale, the Earth will tear away from the Sun’s orbit. Half an hour before the end, the Earth itself will explode from within, as dark energy overcomes the electromagnetic cohesion of molecules.

•	An infinitesimal fraction of a second before the finale, the expansion of space will tear apart the atoms themselves, overcoming the strong nuclear interaction. The Universe will cease its existence, transforming into a ripped fabric of geometry.

**Scenario 3. The Big Crunch (Reverse Singularity)**

This scenario will operate if dark energy eventually squanders its force, its potential drops, and the gravitation of billions of galaxies triumphs over expansion.

•	The Universe will decelerate its flight and begin to mirror-compress back into the past.

•	Galaxies will begin to fall upon one another, space will become compressed, and the plasma temperature will skyrocket to trillions of degrees.

•	In the finale, this entire infinite blueberry pie, along with all its "beads," will compress once more into that very same negligible Planckian point from which everything commenced, closing the vicious circle of broken mathematical equations.

Look at what an astonishing picture this is: official physics invites us to choose exactly how our world will perish — whether it freezes in eternal isolation, tears apart into atoms due to a maddened phantom formula, or burns within a reverse singularity.  
Yet within none of these scenarios is there any location for tangible physical mechanics. We are invited to believe in a balance of forces between gravitation (which in and of itself represents a curvature of emptiness) and dark energy (which is merely a constant number inscribed into equations for the sake of salvaging a theory).

**Conclusion. Where Do the Mathematical Crutches Lead?**

Having traced the entire chronological chain of official cosmology, we can fully appreciate the scale of that grandiose logical trap into which modern theoretical physics has driven itself. In order to salvage a single, initially absurd mathematical dogma — the concept that our entire infinite material world can be birthed out of an infinitesimally small point — science was forced at every turn to construct new invisible entities and prop up the theory with increasingly cumbersome abstract crutches.

Let us look back upon this labyrinth of formulas through which we have passed:

1.	First, Einstein’s equations divide by zero and yield nonsense in the form of infinite density inside a zero volume — the scientific world declares this bug to be a real object named the "Singularity".  
2.	To prevent the singularity from devolving into chaos, 26 independent quantum fields, which are materially vacant yet for some reason do not mix, are packed inside a Planckian diameter (10<sup>-35</sup> meters).  
3.	To explain why the Universe failed to collapse back, they invent a detonator field — the inflaton, which in 10<sup>-32</sup> seconds inflates empty space faster than light. Subsequently, through a mathematical resonance "out of the negative credit of gravitation," it miraculously births real substance by exciting other fields through an unknown mechanism (formulas).  
4.	To prevent this substance from scattering as luminous ghosts, at the mark of 10<sup>-12</sup> seconds inside the volume of the Solar System, they instantaneously turn on the Higgs field. This field "feels the cold" despite having no structure, and endows particles with mass, remaining eternally immutable during an expansion of billions of times.  
5.	To reconcile this nonsense with the observed flat geometry of the cosmos, a single bead is transformed into an infinite garland of concurrently swelling "houses" that expand flush against one another, yet somehow manage not to collide via their boundaries.

To explain the accelerating recession of galaxies in the finale, they introduce dark energy — a mythical "who-knows-what" whose density does not drop upon expansion and which represents merely a dry number inscribed on the margins of equations.

When an ordinary person, liberated from the hypnosis of academic titles, witnesses this avalanche of continuous assumptions, Occam’s razor inevitably triggers: if one must multiply a multitude of invisible miracle-properties to salvage a theory, it implies that the initial model itself is fundamentally erroneous.

Modern cosmology has committed a substitution: it has confused the map with the territory. Mathematical formulas, which were created solely to numerically describe the behavior of matter, were declared the masters of this matter. We have been forced to believe that numbers on paper are capable of creating kilometers, freezing from cold, pushing galaxies apart, and dictating conditions to the physical world. Theoretical physics has transformed into a dry mathematical idealism, completely detached from reality.

Yet real physics has no right to rave. The world is constructed not out of formulas, but out of tangible, extended, and continuous matter. The Universe does not require negative energy credits or the viscous Higgs syrups in order to exist and execute motion.

Within the hypotheses concerning the Big Bang and the future of the Universe, logic and a real physical mechanism are entirely absent from every single thesis of official science. Simply — not from a single one. Criticizing this could fill not just one chapter, but a thick book. I have stopped myself at the first layer of criticism. Will I delve deeper? Yes. But not right now, otherwise this chapter will exceed the volume of the entire rest of the book. There are simply too many punctures in the "logic" of science.

It is precisely this task that Aol physics resolves, returning science from the mad world of mathematical points back onto the solid ground of mechanistic realism. How the Universe is structured within the Aol model, you shall read in the next chapter.





## Chapter 8. AOL SPACE AND THE STRUCTURE OF SMALL UNIVERSES

Having introduced aols as the primary elements of the world, we must now take the next logical step and examine the medium as a continuous whole, rather than focusing on a single, isolated particle. In and of themselves, individual aols cannot yet explain the overarching structure of the Universe. 

They merely provide us with the "substance of space." Our task now is to understand how this substance is organized — why it exists under immense pressure, why it undergoes continuous vibration, and why specific regions of the cosmos foster the conditions for the birth of matter while others do not.

This inquiry brings us to one of the most vital paradigms of the entire aol model: the concept of the Universe as an interconnected system of Mini-Universes — colossal cells of aol space, each governed by its own autonomous internal mechanics.

**1. Inadequacy of the Concept of a Structureless, Infinite Medium**

The simplest hypothesis, often employed by traditional ether theories, postulates that the cosmos is an absolutely homogeneous, infinite, and chaotic array of contiguous particles. However, this view reaches an impasse upon the very first attempt to explain the nature of universal pressure from the perspective of short-range interaction.

If the primordial material medium were infinite and entirely devoid of internal structure, an absolute static equilibrium would be established within a very short time. Within a structureless, isotropic monolith, it is impossible to justify the emergence of stable pressure gradients, variations in physical conditions across different cosmic regions, or the very tendency toward the concentration of forces. An infinite medium without cellular boundaries would inevitably become dead, motionless, and incapable of development.

For the existence of perpetual dynamics, directed flows of momentum, and the continuous movement of matter, a more complex, ordered macro-organization of space is fundamentally required. This is precisely why aol physics asserts that the infinite Universe is structurally segmented into colossal, interconnected, and autonomous blocks — Mini-Universes.

**2. The Physical Nature of the Mini-Universe and the Law of Self-Organization**

The conventional conception of the cosmos as an infinite void in which sparse galaxies and stars are chaotically scattered is deeply erroneous. Chaos at the macro level is merely an illusion caused by the limitations of human perception. Dynamic matter fundamentally strives for equilibrium, which is achieved exclusively through rigid structural ordering.

Self-organization (synergetics) is a basic, inherent property of moving matter. Under the influence of continuous contact collisions, the elements of the medium inevitably rearrange themselves in such a way as to minimize the local tension of internal processes and arrive at the most stable, mechanically favorable state.  
In official physics, this law is described as the principle of energy minimization; in the aol model, however, its essence is expressed more precisely: as a tendency toward the minimization of local motion and pressure.

Examples of such self-organization in continuous media are well known in terrestrial hydrodynamics. A classic example is Rayleigh – Bénard convection cells. When a thin layer of liquid is heated uniformly from below, chaotic convection currents initially arise within it.
However, as the thermal load increases, the system rearranges itself, forming a strict grid of stable hexagonal cells. The physical motion within the medium does not cease; it becomes ordered, because the system finds the optimal spatial form for its existence at a given energy level.

A similar synergetic principle of self-organization operates on the scale of the entire cosmos. Because aol space is hyper-reactive and in continuous contact, absolute static rest within it is impossible. Striving for the most stable dynamic configuration, the gigantic universal array of aols naturally segments into an ordered system of macroscopic cells — Mini-Universes.

The name "Mini-Universe" should not be misleading; it denotes not a physically small scale, but rather its status as a structural building block of the cosmos. Each such cell represents a colossal region of space spanning billions of light-years. It is termed "Mini" solely because it does not constitute the entirety of the infinite Universe, but is merely one local unit within the boundless architecture of the cosmos.

**3. Criteria for Selecting the Geometrical Form of the Cell and the Rhombic Dodecahedron**

Mini-Universes cannot possess an arbitrary geometry, as three fundamental geometric and physical requirements are imposed upon the shape of a spatial cell. 

**First**, it must achieve seamless packing, meaning the figures must tightly fill three-dimensional space without gaps, overlaps, or spaces, entirely eliminating the occurrence of absolute void at the boundaries. 

**Second**, it must maintain dynamic contact, ensuring each cell touches its neighbors in such a way that a stable zone of mutual force balancing and minimal background pressure forms along their shared borders. 

**Third**, it must facilitate a uniform gradient, requiring a shape that provides a natural and isotropic escalation of pressure from the periphery toward the geometric center. Most classical stereometric figures fail to meet these criteria. 

Spheres are ideal for the isotropic transmission of pressure to a center, yet they are fundamentally incapable of filling three-dimensional space without leaving voids at their junctions. 

Cubes provide seamless packing, but due to their sharp vertices and edges, they are incompatible with the concept of uniform central compression.

In aol physics, the unique and natural solution to this problem is the rhombic dodecahedron — a twelve-sided polyhedron with identical rhombic faces. The rhombic dodecahedron stands as a unique volumetric figure capable of executing a tight, gapless packing of three-dimensional space. 

These cells represent a direct, volumetric analogue to the flat hexagons observed in Bénard convection cells. Such a spatial structure is supremely stable, as it allows the system to distribute internal vibrational pressure in the most uniform manner possible.
It is vital to understand that one does not need to assume the medium inherently "knew geometry." 

A stable geometry is born naturally out of the primordial chaos of vibrational pressure regions. This process acts as a mechanical analogue to biological evolution. Configurations incapable of correctly distributing the tensions of the medium are rapidly destroyed or restructured. 

Only those forms that best mitigate internal pressure imbalances survive, endure, and begin to repeat on a massive scale. Thus, the natural selection of forms leaves only the most stable structures within the Universe, and this is precisely how the rhombic dodecahedrons emerged.

Consequently, the Universe is not a collection of random objects, but a singular, self-organizing system in which form, motion, and pressure are deeply intertwined — a system that continuously sustains itself through its own mechanics.

**4. Mechanics of Peripheral Pressure and Cumulative Concentration of Forces Toward the Cell Center**

The starting point for the entire subsequent vibrational mechanics of the cosmos is the initial pressure at the boundaries separating the spatial cells. Because the infinite Universe represents a dense, self-organized system of contacting rhombic dodecahedrons, absolute void is completely absent between the faces of adjacent Mini-Universes. 

In a constant quest for dynamic equilibrium, the hyper-reactive array of aols continuously exchanges vibrations. Supporting one another along their flat boundaries, the Mini-Universes accumulate a mutual force vectors at their junctions. These force vectors compensate for one another, resulting in the lowest possible background pressure for this system being established at the cell's periphery. It is precisely this physical support that enables the generation of the initial baseline pressure. 

One must understand that the peripheries are not merely boundary lines or flat interfacial planes, but rather gigantic regions of space extending for billions of kilometers deep into the interior of the cells.

However, as one advances from the periphery toward the geometric center of the cell, the situation changes fundamentally. The mechanism of pressure escalation is driven by the geometric contraction of the surface area of concentric layers of matter. The vectors of vibrational pressure from the outer boundaries of the cell are transmitted through a chain of continuous collisions deep into the interior of the rhombic dodecahedron. 

At the outermost levels, this total aggregate force is distributed among a colossal number of primary elements. Yet, as the transmission progresses inward, the surface area of each subsequent internal spherical layer of aols inevitably decreases, and consequently, the number of primordial elements constituting that layer is reduced.

An internal mechanical effect of load redistribution arises within the cell, governed by the rule: "the external majority exerts pressure upon the internal minority." A broader outer layer of aols translates its cumulative momentum onto the adjacent inner layer, which consists of fewer particles. 

As a result, each individual aol of the inner level experiences a progressively increasing share of the total pressure of the surrounding medium. This process continues cumulatively all the way to the geometric center of the rhombic dodecahedron. Each deeper layer absorbs the compounding load from all the overlying, highly populated layers of primary matter, naturally transforming the core of the Mini-Universe into a zone of extreme, peak compression and maximum vibration intensity. 

By analogy with the concept of the periphery, it must be understood that the center of a Mini-Universe is a giant region spanning billions of kilometers, rather than a singular, dimensionless geometric point at the center of the rhombic dodecahedron.

**5. The Nature of Universal Micro-Vibration and the Three Factors of Medium Destabilization**

It would be unnatural to assume that the primordial medium of nature exists in a perfectly flawless packing, where every individual aol possesses the maximum number of contacts and the entire structure remains infinitely symmetrical. While such states are convenient for geometry, they are rarely encountered in the real world. 

Natural systems generally form not as idealized blueprints, but as living structures characterized by local deviations, structural imbalances, and a statistical distribution of elements. Therefore, it is far more reasonable to assume that aol space represents not an ideal crystal, but a dense yet incompletely ordered close-packing of aols. 

One aol may have 4 contacts with its neighbors, another 7, a third 10, and some up to 12. Consequently, pressure is distributed unevenly; in certain areas, an aol is compressed more tightly, while in others, it experiences weaker forces. Elsewhere, micro-backlashes occur, and contacts can be nearly direct or aligned at various angles. It is precisely this kind of medium that is capable of possessing internal dynamics, continuous rearrangements, and vibrations.

For this reason, aol space is fundamentally incapable of existing as a static, frozen monolith. Microcosmic vibrations are not an external supplement to pressure — they inevitably flow from it. Within any ultra-dense material medium operating under colossal mechanical tension, the continuous striving to equalize forces combined with the absolute impossibility of achieving total rest invariably generates self-sustained oscillations. 

At its fundamental level, the Universe is permeated by a universal, pervasive background hum. This vibrating array of aols never settles into a state of calm, not only due to the chaotic packing of the particles themselves, but also because the system is continuously driven by three fundamental factors that locally and globally destabilize the structure.

**The first factor** is the emergence of luonols. An initial spherical aol is perfectly integrated into the geometry of the medium, setting aside the chaotic nature of individual contacts. In contrast, a luonol represents a spatially distorted element.   
Its diametric recesses, or cavities, act as geometric defects that locally disrupt the uniform distribution of forces and the symmetry of the surrounding particles' packing. This plastic deformation of the primordial matter makes the medium fundamentally non-ideal. While the reader is not yet familiar with luonols, they will be introduced in detail within the subsequent chapter.

**The second factor** involves the presence of material objects. Any structures built from luonols and aols — ranging from solitary atoms to colossal planets and stars — constitute macroscopic disruptions in the homogeneity of space. They redistribute the flows of elastic collisions, generate local imbalances, and screen forces.   
The larger a material object is, the more severely it deforms the pressure gradient of the surrounding medium. Furthermore, these objects function as aggregators of oscillations, locally escalating the frequency of vibrations.

**The third factor** is the mechanical flattening of luonols at the peripheries of the cells. This process manifests with the highest intensity along the borders of the Mini-Universes. As cosmic objects migrate toward the cell boundaries and disintegrate due to a deficiency in pressure, the luonols that compose them are released from their atomic configurations. Finding itself face-to-face with the free aol medium, the deformed particle undergoes a mechanical processing of colossal proportions — a flattening or reshaping back into a sphere.

The essence of the flattening process is purely impact-driven and high-frequency in nature. The surrounding spherical aols continuously rush into the recesses of the liberated luonol. However, the background pressure at the periphery is insufficient to retain them inside the cavity. 

The spheres that enter the recess are instantaneously ejected by subsequent ricochets, as aols collide with the edges of the cavities and transmit recoil momentum back into the medium. Each such microscopic impact along the edge of the recesses, or within the cavity followed by an immediate ejection, generates a local high-frequency shock, while simultaneously flattening the luonols and restoring their spherical shape. 

Because the mass of the primordial matter being liberated at the outskirts of the cells is colossal, this flow of micro-interactions never ceases for a single moment. The space along the boundaries of the Mini-Universes literally "boils" with billions of elastic micro-collisions occurring at a tremendous frequency on the order of 10<sup>13</sup> Hz.   
The local perturbations from the reshaping luonols accumulate cumulatively and are transmitted through chains of contacting aols back toward the center of the cell, forming the continuous, pervasive vibrational background of the Universe.

**6. The Global Cosmic Conveyor and the Nature of Atomic Vibrations**

The described processes culminate in an infinite, continuously self-regenerating, three-stage cosmological cycle. 

**The first stage** consists of the birth of matter within the central regions of the Mini-Universes. If the pressure of the medium naturally escalates toward the center, then the central domains of the Mini-Universes inevitably become distinct zones of extreme physical conditions. 

Within these colossal spatial volumes, which are entirely baseline-incommensurate with conventional human scales, the external pressure of the medium reaches critical, peak values. It is vital to emphasize that this process does not unfold within an abstract mathematical point of singularity, but rather encompasses giant, real expanses.

It is precisely here, under the influence of monstrous compression, that the fundamental mechanism of aol deformation is triggered. This process does not occur ubiquitously or chaotically, but strictly locally — only where the mechanical load overcomes a specific structural threshold of strength.

Within these extreme central zones, conditions arise under which ordinary aols begin to deform irreversibly, acquiring recesses and transforming into luonols. Prior to this moment, as long as the spatial pressure remains moderate, the aols retain their original smooth, spherical shape. 

In this state, they are capable only of translating vibrations and transmitting pressure, but are fundamentally unable to form mechanical interlocks with one another. Only the ultimate compression at the cores of the Mini-Universes forces the primary elements of the medium to alter their geometry. The emergence of the recesses fundamentally changes the rules of the game, unlocking the physical possibility for the assembly of the first stable LEGO-like structures: atoms, molecules, and all subsequent tangible matter.

From this rigorous mechanics, it logically follows that the matter familiar to us does not exist eternally in a ready-made and immutable form. It is a product of the evolution of space itself, born exclusively within the dedicated factories of the aol medium where the necessary conditions have been established.

Thus, matter is generated not by a mystical prime mover, but by the vibrating medium itself, which, through the sheer force of its own pressure, forges stable geometric elements out of spherical aols.

**The second stage** involves the movement from the center to the periphery. The pressure gradient, which reaches its maximum at the center and decreases toward the outskirts, generates a constant centrifugal force, causing any complex material object to inevitably experience a vector expulsion outward. 

Newly formed matter does not remain forever in the central domain, as the vibrating medium itself continues to exert a continuous and strictly directed influence upon it. The escalation of spatial density toward the center creates a steady tendency of extrusion for all already established structures. 

Matter is literally pushed out from the zone of maximum compression into regions with a lower density of aols. This natural centrifugal drift of objects acts as the basic macroscopic "current" of the Universe, which will subsequently play a key role in explaining large-scale cosmic movements and governing galactic dynamics. 

Thus, the Mini-Universe functions as a cyclic dynamic system in which the medium first concentrates pressure toward the center to assemble material elements, and then systematically evacuates the results of this assembly to its periphery.

**The Third Stage: Disintegration of Matter at the Periphery and Completion of the Cosmic Cycle**

Within the peripheral sectors of the Mini-Universe cells, a physical process unfolds that operates in a manner directly inverse to the central assembly. As centrifugal displacement forces cosmic objects away from the center toward the marginal zones of low pressure, the external compression of the medium drops beneath a critical threshold, rendering it insufficient to maintain complex material structures in a stable configuration. Within these rarified domains, conditions materialize for the inevitable weakening, degradation, and disintegration of matter. 

Intricate atomic LEGO-like architectures fracture, liberating the constituent luonols.
