The directed helical motion of the "serpent" is hindered, the vibration of the atoms grows, and the heating of the conductor increments. The more geometric obstacles reside in the path of the "serpent," the higher the resistance of the material and the stronger its heating.

From this, the fundamental distinction between conductors, insulators, and semiconductors automatically becomes clear:

**•	In metals,** the geometry of the atomic configurations initially forms free, through corridor-channels for the unhindered sliding of the aol chain. Current flows easily.  
**•	In dielectrics (insulators)**, these channels are ironcladly blocked by the protruding elements of the atoms — the "serpent" is physically incapable of squeezing through the atoms; it is ruptured into fragments.  
**•	In semiconductors,** we possess an intermediate geometry: channels exist, yet they open or close depending upon external conditions (temperature, pressure, or illumination — the latter triggers an additional high-frequency bombardment of the semiconductor's material, reconfiguring its structure and shifting parts of the atoms).

Furthermore, if we commence to cool the metal to critical thresholds, the thermal chaos inside it will subside. The atomic configurations practically freeze in their positions (the amplitude of their vibrations diminishes), ceasing to sway from side to side, and all obstacles for the aol "serpent" vanish completely; the channels become fixed. The serpent commences to slide through the metal with zero losses whatsoever, and this current can flow indefinitely. It is precisely in this manner that the mechanics of Aol physics explains the phenomenon of **superconductivity.**

**The Magnetic Field and Induction**

When the aol "serpent" pushes helically inside a conductor, by virtue of spatial tightness and geometry, it inevitably acts upon the mobile elements of the atomic configurations. Components of the atoms commence to intensively rotate, and this local rotation is instantaneously transmitted outward, capturing the free aols of the surrounding space and twisting them into powerful circular vortices-whirlpools around the wire. This is precisely that which science terms a magnetic field. This represents not an abstract, immaterial "force field" of an unknown nature, but a real, tangible circular motion of the aol medium.

Such a mechanistic approach, devoid of abstractions, explains **Ampere’s Law:**

**•	If the current flows in a single direction through two parallel conductors:** the aol vortices around them rotate coordinately in a single direction. A certain portion of the vortex becomes common to both conductors, rotating around both wires — it closes into a unified, coordinated vortex. In the center between the conductors, the vortices mutually cancel out, attenuating the pressure. As a result, the external aol space physically presses the conductors toward one another.

**•	If the currents are directed in opposite directions:** their aol vortices fail to construct a common vortex — they are directed oppositely, encounter-wise, yet both vortices inside the center between the conductors coordinately "pump" an excess of aols. A zone of elevated pressure arises between the conductors, which pushes the conductors apart toward the sides with force in search of a pressure equilibrium.

**The famous electromagnetic induction** is a direct consequence of this exact mechanics of contact and pressure. When the current inside the first conductor alters its velocity or direction, the dynamics of the twisted aol vortices around it alters instantaneously. These altering vortices of rotation reach the adjacent wire and commence to mechanically rock the elements of its constituent atoms. The rotating atoms of the second conductor, in their turn, catch and push the internal aol "serpent" inside its channels. A current emerges within the adjacent circuit.

There is zero mystical "manifestation of energy out of a vacuum" or contactless sorcery here. There is exclusively a strict adherence to the fundamental law of motion: the direct mechanical transmission of momentum and pressure from one material point to another through an elastic medium.

**Direct Current — and Why It Fails to Create Induction**

When direct current flows through a conductor, stable vortex motions of the aol space are formed around it. These vortices are steady. They are directed identically, their velocity is immutable, and they do not alter over time. The surrounding medium rapidly arrives at an equilibrium state.

If a second conductor resides adjacent, its constituent atoms inevitably fall under the mechanical impact of these twisted streams. The mobile components of their atomic configurations obediently rotate, adjust to the vector of the medium's motion, occupy a new fixed position — and upon that, everything finishes. The system has arrived at a mechanical equilibrium. Zero alteration of interaction implies zero further motion, which implies zero current within the second conductor.

It is precisely for this reason that direct current is physically incapable of creating a sustained induction. It merely once — at the instant of switching on — reconfigures the geometry of the adjacent conductor via its vortex and ironcladly locks it within that position, having generated a brief, one-time push of the "serpent".

**Alternating Current — This Is Where Induction Manifests**

Now let us examine alternating current.  
When the current within the first conductor alters its direction, the direction of the aol space's vortices twist concurrently and instantaneously. The medium commences to continuously, actively reconfigure — first in a solitary direction, and subsequently in the opposite one.

And this is a fundamental point.    
The atoms of the second conductor inside such an altering stream can no longer occupy a static, steady position. Their mobile elements are forced to continuously — in lockstep with the source — displace following the altering vortices of the medium: first thither, and subsequently backward. This oscillatory interaction from without commences to push the internal aol "serpent" of the second wire forward and backward. Within the closed circuit of the second conductor, an alternating current emerges.

It is precisely upon this principle that the transformer operates. Inside the primary winding, an alternating current is created, forming constantly altering vortices of the aol space. These vortices act upon the secondary winding, forcing its constituent atoms to continuously reconfigure. As a result, an alternating current emerges within the secondary winding — devoid of direct contact between the windings, but rather through the medium.

Induction motion arises by no means because a mythical "mathematical field" creates something out of a void. It arises because the elastic medium fails to permit the physical system to arrive at rest: it reconfigures all the time, and by virtue of this, continuously transmits a pure mechanical momentum further.

A simple, distinct regularity is visible here:  
**•	Constant interaction** fixes the structure.  
**•	Altering interaction** pushes it.  
**•	A constant shift in direction** creates a steady, continuous oscillatory motion.

**Summary**

Voltage is the pressure differential of the aol medium at the ends of a conductor.  
Electrical current is the directed motion of the aols inside a conductor.  
Resistance represents losses during the transmission of motion through the atomic structure.  
A magnetic field consists of the circular vortices of the aols around a conductor.  
Induction is the interaction of aol vortices upon an adjacent conductor and the creation of a directed motion of the aols within it.

Electromagnetic waves represent the transmission of oscillations from a source of oscillations via the aols of the aol space. These waves are not transverse, but longitudinal. These are mechanical waves of compression and rarefaction of the aol space. The velocity of electromagnetic waves — 300,000 kilometers per second — is ensured exclusively by the properties of the aol space: by the rigidity of the aols and the density of the aol space. Absolutely everything transmitted through the medium invariably possesses the speed of light.

What can be stated concerning electrical charges — positive and negative? One can state that zero mysterious charges, whose physical essence is absolutely unknown, exist in nature. Inside itself, matter possesses neither energy, nor charges, nor anything else. Matter is passive. Yet it can execute motion in different ways (if it is pushed). Conversely, it can execute motion structurally, in an ordered fashion, and then there will be an electrical current or a magnetic field.

Furthermore, it can execute motion rotationally—clockwise or counter-clockwise. And inside this case, to such a rotational motion, one can artificially assign a positive or a negative "charge." And these "charges" will be pressed together or repelled, depending upon the coordinated or uncoordinated encounter motion of the twisted space's aol "vortices."

In the end, we witness one and the same mechanism everywhere: contact, pressure, motion. Electricity ceases to be an enigma and becomes that which it actually is — the mechanics of the aol medium, or aol-dynamics.

The might of electrical processes is explained not by the enigmatic properties of rare invisible particles barely crawling inside a void, but by the properties of a dense, continuous medium surrounding and permeating all substance, and capable of transmitting colossal mechanical interactions. ## Chapter 36. ELECTROMAGNETIC WAVES — THE TRANSMISSION OF VIBRATIONS

When it comes to electromagnetic waves, modern physics confidently uses formulas and terminology. But if we ask: what exactly is moving? — no clear mechanical answer is given.

We are told that an electromagnetic wave propagates.
We are told that an electric field generates a magnetic field, and a magnetic field generates an electric field.

But what are these fields?
What are they made of?
What exactly is oscillating?
What transmits the effect from point to point?
There is no answer in the mechanical sense.

Words are used, mathematics is used, but the carrier is not described, contact is not described, pressure is not described. That fundamental triad required to understand a process is missing.

In the aol model, everything becomes simpler.
An electromagnetic wave is not something special. It is the same process as light, but at different frequencies.
It is the transmission of vibrations through aol space.
Nothing is flying anywhere.
No particles are being emitted.
No “fields” are running through space on their own.

There are only vibrations of the medium.

Let us consider the source — an antenna.
When alternating current flows in an antenna, the atoms of the conductor begin to oscillate. These oscillations are transmitted to aol space — the atoms literally “strike” the aols, creating a sequence of compressions and expansions in the medium.

Each aol, receiving the impact, passes it on to neighboring aols.
This is how the wave arises.
It is not a transverse wave as typically depicted in textbooks. It is a longitudinal wave — a wave of compression and expansion, like sound, but propagating not in air, but in aol space.

Why is the speed of this wave equal to the speed of light?
Because the mechanism is the same.
Both light and electromagnetic waves are transmissions of vibrations from aol to aol. The speed is determined by the properties of the aols themselves — their stiffness, their ability to deform, and the rate at which they transmit impacts.

The source may differ, the frequencies may differ, but the transmission mechanism is the same.
That is why the speed is the same.
The difference between light and radio waves is not in their nature, but in frequency.
High-frequency vibrations are perceived by the eye as light.
Low-frequency vibrations are radio waves, invisible to the eye but detectable by antennas.
Their nature is the same — vibrations of the medium.

Thus, there is no need to introduce mysterious entities:

there is no need to explain how an electric field “creates” a magnetic field,

no need to explain how “empty space oscillates,”

no need to invent what moves in the absence of a carrier.

There is a medium.
There are vibrations.
There is transmission of motion.
And that is enough.

Electromagnetic waves are not a special phenomenon. They are a natural extension of the same mechanism we have already seen in light, heat, and electricity.
They are simply vibrations of aol space.

**Antenna — Geometry of Interaction with the Medium**

In modern physics, an antenna is usually described in terms of parameters, impedance, matching, and radiation patterns. All of this works, but if we ask: what does an antenna physically do? — there is again no mechanical answer.

In the aol model, the answer is simple.
An antenna is a device that strikes the aol space in a specific way.
When alternating current flows through an antenna, its atoms begin to oscillate. These oscillations are transmitted into aol space. But the transmission does not occur arbitrarily — it follows the geometry of the conductor.

And here, shape becomes the key factor.
If we take a straight conductor, its atoms oscillate along its length. This means that impacts on aol space occur primarily along that axis. As a result, a directed propagation of vibrations is formed — the antenna begins to “radiate” in specific directions.

If the antenna is bent, formed into a loop, or has a complex shape, the pattern of impacts on the medium changes. Vibrations are transmitted differently, and the wave acquires a different geometry.
Thus, an antenna does not “emit a wave” — it defines the geometry of vibration transmission in the medium.

This also explains another important aspect — antenna length.
Why are antennas made with lengths related to wavelength?
Because an antenna operates efficiently when its dimensions are matched to the frequency of oscillations.
If the antenna length corresponds to half a wavelength (or a multiple of it), the oscillations of its atoms combine coherently. Impacts on aol space reinforce each other rather than cancel out. The transmission becomes maximally efficient.

If the length is not matched, different parts of the antenna operate out of phase. Some regions reinforce the vibrations, others partially cancel them. Efficiency drops sharply.
Thus, an antenna is not just a piece of metal — it is a resonant system matched to the oscillations of the medium.

Even more simply:
An antenna is a tool that: defines the direction of impacts on the medium, defines their frequency, ensures their coherence.

That is why it can both transmit and receive.
When external vibrations of aol space reach the antenna, they begin to act on its atoms. If the shape and size of the antenna are matched to these vibrations, the atoms begin to oscillate in resonance. The aol “snake” begins to move — a current appears in the antenna.

The antenna converts vibrations of the medium into motion within the conductor.
And vice versa.
Motion in the conductor is converted into vibrations of the medium.
It is the same process — just in two directions.

**Radio Communication — Transmission of Vibrations Through a Medium**

Radio communication may seem almost magical: one device “sends a signal,” another, located hundreds or thousands of kilometers away, “receives” it.
In the classical description, it is said that an “electromagnetic wave” is radiated into space, carrying “energy and information.” 

But what exactly is being transmitted?
What moves from one antenna to another?
What carries the information?
Where is this carrier?
There is no answer in the mechanical sense.

There are formulas, calculations, and working technology — but no description of what physically happens.

In the aol model, this gap disappears.
Radio communication is not the transmission of “something through emptiness.”
It is the transmission of vibrations through a medium.

Let us begin with the transmitter.
An alternating current is created in the transmitter. But by itself, it does not yet carry information. Information appears when we begin to modify this current — its amplitude, frequency, or phase.
This means the antenna begins to oscillate not just rhythmically, but with a changing pattern of motion.
The atoms of the antenna strike the aol space, creating waves of compression and expansion. But now these oscillations carry structure — variations in which the information is encoded.

Next comes propagation.
Vibrations are transmitted from aol to aol. Nothing flies. No particles leave the antenna and travel through space.
Only motion is transmitted.
The medium simply reproduces, step by step, what was initiated at the source.
These vibrations reach the receiving antenna.

Now the reverse process occurs.
The aol space begins to act on the atoms of the antenna. If the antenna is tuned to the frequency of the incoming vibrations, its atoms begin to oscillate in resonance.

The aol “snake” begins to move — an electric current arises.
But this current is not random. It already contains the same pattern of variations that was encoded in the transmitter. The circuitry extracts these variations — and we hear sound, see images, or receive data.

Now we can state the key point.
Radio communication is not the transfer of a signal.
It is the reproduction of motion.
The transmitter defines motion in the medium.
The medium transmits this motion.
The receiver reproduces it.
There is no discontinuity in this process.

Now consider it from a common-sense perspective.
How can “emptiness” carry anything?
How can a “field” exist without a carrier?
How can transmission occur without contact?
To transmit motion, a carrier is required.
To exert influence, contact is required.
For something to happen, pressure is required.

And that is exactly what we see.
The transmitting antenna exerts pressure on the medium.
The medium transmits this pressure.
The receiving antenna converts it back into motion.
Everything fits into one simple scheme: contact — pressure — motion.
Nothing more.

Radio communication ceases to be mysterious once we stop asking “what is flying” and instead examine how motion is transmitted.
And then it becomes clear: there is no “jump through emptiness,” no “field magic” — only continuous transmission of vibrations through aol space.

**Spectrum of Vibrations — A Unified Scale**

In physics, when we speak of radio waves, light, ultraviolet radiation, or X-rays, they are usually presented as different phenomena grouped into the “electromagnetic spectrum.”

But if we think carefully, the same question arises: what exactly differs?
In the aol model, the answer is extremely simple.
What differs is the frequency of vibrations.
And nothing else.

In all these cases, the same process occurs — the transmission of vibrations through aol space. The only difference is how often the micro-impacts occur, how frequently the medium compresses and expands.

If the frequencies are low — these are radio waves.  
If higher — infrared vibrations, perceived as heat.  
Higher still — visible light.  
Higher — ultraviolet.  
Then X-rays and the gamma range.

But the nature of all these phenomena is the same.
There are no separate “types of waves.”
There are no different entities for radio, light, and X-rays.
There is only a single scale of vibrations in the medium.

It is like sound.
Low frequencies are bass, high frequencies are high-pitched tones — but the medium is the same (air), and the mechanism is the same: compression and expansion.
The same applies here.

Aol space is a single medium.
Vibrations are a single process.
Frequency is the only changing parameter.
This clarifies an important point.
Why does the eye see only a narrow range?
Because the eye is a receiver tuned to specific frequencies.
This is not a property of “light.”
It is a property of the eye.
If evolution had taken a different path, we might “see” radio waves as light or perceive X-rays as heat. But nature has given us only a narrow sensory range.

Everything else is the same vibrations — just outside our perception.
There is another important conclusion.
The higher the frequency of vibrations, the stronger their effect on matter.
Because impacts occur more frequently.
Low frequencies gently oscillate atoms, while high-frequency vibrations begin to strongly affect their structure. They can break bonds, alter atomic configurations and destroy matter.

In the aol model, this is explained simply: not as “more energetic quanta,” but as more frequent and intense воздействия of the medium.
Thus, the entire “electromagnetic spectrum” is not a set of different phenomena, but a unified picture.
One process. One medium. One nature.
Only the frequency changes.
And once this is understood, there is no need to divide the world into “radio,” “light,” and “radiation.”
There are only vibrations of aol space — from slow to extremely fast.






## Chapter 37. FIELDS

**Fields: Science’s Attempt to Animate the Void**

**1. What a Field Represents in the Concept of Science: Fleeing from Absolute Nothingness**

To comprehend why official physics devised the concept of "fields" in the first place, one must return to the era when science believed in an absolute void. When Isaac Newton discovered the law of universal gravitation, he was confronted by the harshest criticism from his contemporaries. Newton asserted that the Sun attracts the Earth across millions of kilometers of cosmic vacuum.

Common sense and logic cried out: how can one object interact with another if there is absolutely nothing between them? If space is empty, then a push or a pull cannot be transmitted, for they lack a mediator. Newton himself acknowledged this dead end, declaring that "action-at-a-distance through a void" is an absurdity that no person with a philosophical mindset could ever believe.

Physics found itself on the brink of collapse: the equations functioned, but the physical mechanism was absent. And then, in the 19th century, Michael Faraday and James Clerk Maxwell proposed a saving sophisic patch — the concept of the **"Field."**

Science declared: space as an absolute "nothingness" no longer exists. Space is filled with invisible, intangible structures — fields. The Earth does not simply attract the Moon across a distance. The Earth generates a gravitational field around itself, and it is this field, residing here and now at the exact location of the Moon, that physically acts upon it.

It appeared as though materialism had triumphed — the void had been filled with a medium. Yet official science immediately performed a logical somersault, retreating into mysticism. Having declared the field to be the mediator of interaction, it flatly refused to endow this field with material properties. A field in official physics is not a gas, not a liquid, and not a crystal lattice. It is a disembodied spirit that possesses force, yet lacks mass, structure, and any primary foundation.

**2. Born of Madness: The Big Bang and the Paradox of Field Expansion**

To legalize the existence of disembodied fields, official cosmology devised a monumental myth — the Big Bang theory. We are told that roughly 14 billion years ago, our entire gigantic Universe, along with all its billions of galaxies, was compressed into a single "singularity" — an insane mathematical point with zero volume and infinite density. In this point, there were no atoms, no space, and no time. There was merely a primordial soup of pure energy and fields. We have already spoken of energy and we know that it does not exist — neither pure, nor dark, nor any other kind — it is merely an entry in a notebook.

Then this point exploded and began to expand. And here, official science enters a severe logical dead end:  
**•	The Paradox of Rarefaction:**  
Any real, tangible matter (gas, liquid, powder), when its volume increases, inevitably becomes rarefied. Its density drops, and its bonds weaken. Were fields a material medium, then as the Universe expanded by billions of billions of times, they would have had to "thin out," their elasticity would have had to plummet, and their properties and fundamental constants would have had to alter radically.

**•	How Science Wriggles Out:**  
To prevent their formulas from collapsing, physicists declared: space expands, but the properties of fields are born absolutely perfect and immutable at any new point. Fields do not stretch like rubber — they literally clone themselves out of nothing, preserving their "density" in every single cubic centimeter of the cooling cosmos. Official physics has substituted the mechanical process of a medium's expansion with the miracle of the continuous spontaneous generation of fields.

To comprehend just how deeply modern academic science has become mired in this dead end, it is sufficient to pose to official physics two of the simplest materialist questions regarding fields: "At what exact moment did they appear?" and "Out of what are they made?"

The answers you will find in textbooks on Quantum Field Theory (QFT) are capable of shocking any person possessing common sense. Official science asserts in all seriousness: fields are made of nothing and have always existed.

**Of What Are the "Fields of Science" Woven?**

Science has officially rejected a material carrier. For an academic physicist, a field is an immaterial property of the void itself. Scientists offer us the following speculative construct: imagine that absolutely empty space is an infinite computer canvas. At every geometric point of this canvas, an abstract numerical function (a vector or a matrix) is "programmed". This number does not signify the density or pressure of real particles. It signifies merely the mathematical "degree of readiness" of the void to birth a particle at that given point.

If one were to "pump abstract energy" into this digital grid, the numerical value at that point would increase. The field would become "excited," and physicists would term this mathematical excitement a real, tangible electron or photon. That is to say, in the comprehension of QFT, a field is a disembodied software code of the Universe, existing entirely on its own. Particles in such physics are not rigid bodies, but merely "splashes" and "waves" upon the surface of this digital ocean of emptiness.

**A Second Before the Blast: Where Were the Fields Sleeping?** 

An even greater logical nightmare commences when academic science attempts to reconcile its non-material fields with the concept of the Big Bang and singularity. Here, official cosmology splits into two equally fantastical camps:

**•	Version A (The Primordial Fields):**  
Quantum fields existed even before the Big Bang in a state of the so-called "false vacuum". Space did not yet exist, substance was absent, meters and seconds did not signify anything, yet an abstract inflaton field was already hanging somewhere. Then, a mathematical glitch (a quantum fluctuation) occurred within this primordial code, which spun the flywheel of the Big Bang, birthing the physical world. Fields here are the non-material parents of the material Universe.

**•	Version B (The Fracture of the Super-field):**  
Fields turned on simultaneously with spacetime during the so-called Planck epoch **(10<sup>-43</sup> seconds)**. Into an infinitely small point of singularity that possesses zero volume, science managed to "cram" the so-called Super-unification — a single, Solitary, Unified Super-field.  
As the void born from that point expanded and cooled, this Super-field began to "crack" and delaminate, resembling a cooling frost pattern on winter glass. First, gravitation split off, followed by the strong nuclear field, and at the very end, the Higgs field activated, which began to mechanically brake the flying quanta, thereby endowing them with mass.

**A Trap for the Academicians**

The attempt to cram quantum fields into a singular point exposes the complete collapse of official mathematics. By the laws of that very same quantum physics, fields execute micro-oscillations even at absolute rest. Every single oscillation carries a tiny energy. Yet because there is an infinite number of points in space, the cumulative energy of fields in the Universe equals infinity.

When Einstein’s General Relativity attempts to compress all of space along with these fields into a single dimensionless point that is smaller than an electron, the energy density becomes "infinity to the power of infinity."  The mathematics yields a division by zero. Furthermore, Heisenberg’s quantum principle explicitly prohibits compressing a field into a zero volume — the tighter you constrain it, the more powerfully and chaotically it explodes.

Consequently, we possess a paradox: fields are supposed to exist inside a point, yet a geometric point possesses neither volume nor a coordinate grid to contain these fields within itself. Physicists cannot coherently explain what exactly resided inside this point, what forced it to "explode," and where the laws of nature were stored when space itself did not yet exist.

From this conceptual dead end, official science possesses only a single exit — to declare that at the point of the Big Bang, "the familiar laws of physics simply cease to operate." Where the logic of abstract formulas breaks down, academic science simply abolishes physics itself.

The Big Bang concept is the logical finale of a mathematical phantasmagoria. The entirety of modern cosmology has transformed into a religion where the Singularity acts in place of God, and equations yielding infinities upon any attempt to verify them with common sense act in place of sacred texts. Absurdity wrapped in complex tensor analysis does not cease to be an absurdity. It merely becomes difficult to access for criticism.

**3. The Communal Apartment of the Vacuum: 25 Layers at a Single Point**

While in the 19th century scientists spoke merely of gravitation and electromagnetism, the modern Standard Model of particle physics has multiplied this list to the point of absurdity. As of today, science counts 25 fundamental fields. There are quark fields (for each of the 6 types of quarks), lepton fields (the electron, muon, and tau fields, along with three types of neutrino fields), gauge fields (the photon and gluon fields, and the fields of the W and Z bosons), and the Higgs field.

And it is precisely here that human common sense is confronted by a phenomenal violation of the principle of "where are you going? This spot is occupied!":  
•	In the macro-world, two objects cannot occupy the exact same location. You cannot place a chair where a wardrobe already stands.

•	In official physics, all 25 fields simultaneously, irrevocably, and indivisibly occupy every single microscopic point of space in the Universe.  
They do not displace one another in any way, they do not rub against each other, and they do not hinder one another's existence. Inside one and the same cubic millimeter of vacuum, 25 independent "pieces" of fields are concurrently "poured."

This is not an elastic medium; it is a mystical communal apartment where dozens of disembodied entities reside upon the exact same area, completely ignoring the geometry of spatial tightness. They are not adjacent to one another — they are inside one another. Inside one another at every single minuscule point of the Universe's space. At every single point and within every single volume — from the minute to the cosmic.  
It is vital to hear this. To comprehend this is impossible, given that it is an absurdity, yet it must be heard. Pause your reading and ponder this absurdity.

**4. The Higgs Field: The Immutable Molasses of the Vacuum**

Among these 25 entities, there is a distinct "celebrity" of modern physics — the Higgs field. Official science declares that it is precisely this field which is responsible for the fact that our world possesses mass.

**•	The official mechanism:**  
Space is filled with an invisible Higgs field that behaves like an infinite, viscous cosmic molasses. When an electron or a quark flies, it forces its way through this field; the field brakes it, and it is this resistance that official science terms "mass." Were the Higgs field absent, particles would fly at the speed of light, and the world would be unable to assemble into atoms.

**•	The Absurdity of Invariance:**  
The Universe expands with acceleration, and galaxies scatter apart, yet the density of the Higgs field remains absolutely immutable since the time of the Big Bang. Space grows, but the density of this "molasses" does not drop by a single iota. Furthermore, this Higgs field itself possesses no direction — it is a scalar field. It simply "exists" at all points without having any material carrier particles in a stable state. Science invented an invisible syrup that eternally generates a force of friction for particles, yet itself never diminishes and consists of nothing.

**•	A Blow to Logic:**  
If the Higgs field is a braking medium (molasses), then during any braking, according to mechanics, energy must be liberated (heat, radiation, wear). Where does the energy of an electron's friction against the Higgs "molasses" go? Why does the electron not heat up and why does it not lose its velocity over time if it has been forcing its way through a viscous field for billions of years? Science is silent, because it lacks any mechanism for this "braking without energy loss."

**5. The Pedigree of the Micro-World: Who Is the Parent — the Particle or the Field?**
 
Within official physics, a dramatic reversal of roles has occurred, which has definitively entangled the logic of cause-and-effect relations.

**•	How it was previously (The logic of relatively "common" sense):**  
Physicists considered the particle (the electron) to be the parent and the field to be the child. There is a material electron — it generates an electrical field around itself via its charge. There is no electron — the field has vanished. The cause births the effect.

**•	How it has become now (The absurdity of Quantum Field Theory):**  
Science has declared the field to be the parent and the particle to be the effect. The electron field is primary; it has always existed everywhere. Meanwhile, the electron itself is merely a local "splash," a quantum excitation of this field. But how, then, does a charged body generate an electrical field? The child births the parent once again? Utter confusion...

If the field birthed the particle, how then does this particle manage to execute motion?  
In QFT, a flying electron is not a sphere flying through a field. It is when the field at point (A) ceases to oscillate, while at the neighboring point (B) it commences oscillation. The "motion" of a particle in official physics is merely the sequential flickering and leaping of an excitation from one sector of an invisible field to another. The material object is erased. The world is transformed into a Christmas garland where the lightbulbs do not run along the wire but simply ignite in turn, creating the illusion of running.

Yet if an electron is merely the "blinking" of a field, what forces this blinking to displace? What force transmits the "baton of oscillation" from point (A) to point (B)? If the field is identical everywhere, it possesses no built-in motor that would drive a wave in a specific direction. Science has substituted the physical flight of a particle with the mystical self-propagation of a wave of probability.

**6. Spacetime: The Bending of Non-Existent Linearity**

The ultimate apex of rejecting materiality became the symbiosis of space and time within Albert Einstein’s General Relativity (GR). To explain gravitation without involving a material ether or an elastic medium, science declared: space and time do not exist separately; there is a unified four-dimensional continuum — Spacetime.  
And this continuum, according to GR, can be curved.

**•	Where the anti-logic lies:**  
When the Earth orbits the Sun, it flies not because it is pulled by a material force. It flies along a straight line, but the very space surrounding the Sun has "sagged" like a mattress under a heavy person. Furthermore, it flies under the action of a mysterious inertia that possesses no explanation.

**•	The Paradox of Time Curvature:**  
Time is irrevocably woven into this four-dimensional curved mattress. GR asserts that gravity (heavy mass) curves not only lengths but concurrently slows the very passage of time. A logical question arises: what is the mechanical tool of this curvature?

What is the mechanical tool of this curvature? Time is a measure of the duration of processes; it is not a substance. Space in official physics is geometry, an extension in which there is no matter (since fields consist of nothing). It turns out that a heavy body (the Sun), by its very presence, curves geometric emptiness and mathematical duration.

Official science cannot demonstrate a single gear, a single thread, or a single vector of real pressure that could physically bend the coordinate of time. We are slipped a beautiful mathematical metaphor of the "warping of a fabric," while it is concealed from us that this fabric does not possess a single material fiber. If space is nothingness, then it possesses no internal rigidity. And if it lacks rigidity, it is fundamentally impossible to "bend" or "straighten" it — for bending requires the resistance of a medium. Einstein forced the world to believe in the elasticity of a void.

They employ the word "fabric," yet they fail to describe of what it consists and how a body is capable of curving it. They draw a trampoline, but a trampoline possesses matter; everything with it is straightforward, and it sags in a single direction. Yet, in what direction does the "trampoline" of space sag? Space, after all, is volumetric, not flat like a strictly oriented trampoline. It would simply collapse entirely inward.

*Author's Commentary:* Incidentally, this is precisely how official science explains it: what rolls bodies along their orbits is not the bending of space (since this bending would force bodies to roll strictly into the center of the mass that is warping the space), but the "bending" of time—the flight of a body from the past into the future. Why should a body fly along a curve from the past into the future?

In general, the mechanism of depressing a volumetric space consisting of who-knows-what, along with this astonishing flight from the past into the future — which supposedly forces a body not to slide down the curvature toward the center of the gravitating mass, but to fly around it—remains completely incomprehensible. It would be highly curious to look upon a human who actually understands this.

**7. Mathematical Dictate Instead of Physical Reality**

And here we approach the moment of the final exposure of the official concept of fields. Ponder this absurdity! We are literally being told that a field is simply when a number is recorded at every point in space. If we speak of an electrical field, it is a force vector; if of a gravitational field, it is a curvature tensor.

Yet a number, a vector, and a function are merely entries in a human’s notebook. A number cannot physically attract a planet. A vector drawn on paper cannot deflect a flying body. A mathematical function possesses no mass, no elasticity, and no capability to transmit a mechanical impulse.

Physics has completely replaced matter with mathematics. A field in official textbooks is not a physical "dough"; it is a space in which numbers alter by the will of equations. Science has constructed a perfect abstract castle that aligns flawlessly in the notebooks of calculators, yet possesses not the slightest relation to the search for the real physical mechanism of the Universe.

### Aol Physics on Fields

Humans, in order to make something, execute a vast mass of technological processes. Nature, to make something at the macro-level, presses, blows, washes with water, and interacts via temperature.

What is changing into what, and by virtue of what? What executes the actions to perform this transformation?

How does one thing generate something else out of itself, only for the reverse process to occur afterward? Once again, we must recall the process of manufacturing (making).

And on top of that, it hurtles along at the speed of light. While traveling at the speed of light, it somehow manages to re-manufacture things — converting one into the other and back again. Ponder the core essence of these questions.

The very moment you attempt to visualize what exactly exists in space that transforms it into a magnetic field — meaning you have reserved this pocket of space for your specific piece of a field and are contemplating how this pocket transforms from a magnetic "something" into an electrical "something" — I cram another wave into this exact same pocket of space, which demands its own "something," and then more and more waves into the very same location...

With what do they execute it? With what kind of "hands"? With what kind of "machines"? One cannot simply say, "it makes."

What am I attempting to convey by posing these questions, to which no one possesses even a hint of an answer? I am attempting to convey that a field is an absolutely incomprehensible physical state of space. There is not even a hint of understanding. Only wild fantasies devoid of any mechanism's explanation.

Having read all of this, attempt to answer the question: what is a field? Not a formula. Not an equation.
